# Era-Neutral Reward Catalog

*Generated 2026-10-08 by `ShardContent/tools/cosmetic-catalog/catalog.py build --neutral`. Edit the category files in `catalog-neutral/`, not this page.*

**925 rewards in 30 categories.** Machine-readable copy: [`cosmetic-rewards-neutral.json`](cosmetic-rewards-neutral.json). Every reward has a permanent id `CN-<CAT>-<NNN>`; the `CN-` prefix marks them as era-neutral so a system that must stay strictly inside April 2000 can ignore them by prefix.

> **What era neutral means here.** These rewards fall *outside* the main catalog's era grading (`Cosmetic-Reward-Catalog.md`): their art, or the object itself, first appeared after April 2000 (or was never documented for the era). They are kept because the *thing* is generic: nothing in its look or lore ties it to a later expansion, creature, race, skill or event, so it would not seem out of place in 2000 Britannia (a jack-o'-lantern, a weapon rack, a ship's wheel, a mirror, or necromancy-flavoured props such as skulls and jars of eyeballs). Items that *do* belong to later content (samurai or ninja pieces, elven or gargoyle furnishings, figurines of creatures that did not exist, gear for skills the era lacks) are deliberately not here. Each entry's era note says why it is neutral and when its art first appeared (`artEra`).

> **Felucca note.** Felucca runs the client's *Desolation* season, which hides every tile flagged *Foliage* (leaf canopies, many bushes). Entries that contain such tiles say so below and show an *as seen in Felucca* picture; the owner can change the map season if full leaves are wanted. The in-game gallery shots in `client-shots/` are taken in Felucca and show exactly what a player sees.
